@@ -34,7 +35,7 @@

 
 1. Context Diagram
 
-The WMSD supports internal tile-warehouse operations: access control, products and lots, suppliers, replenishment, purchase orders, receiving and inspection, storage locations, quality-specific balances, stock movements, stocktake, goods issue, returns, reports and lot traceability. External suppliers and customers are represented by source documents, not by assumed portal-user accounts.
+WMSD serves exactly five application roles: Store Owner, Accountant, Inventory Staff, Sales Staff and Administrator. Suppliers and customers are external business parties, not additional application roles. WMSD includes Sales Orders, Outbound Deliveries, picking Transfer Orders, Post Goods Issue, internal invoice issuance with PDF/print output, SMTP order/invoice status notifications, customer and supplier returns, and approval and evidence recording for externally completed customer-return refunds. Master data and manually entered selling prices are included; any margin calculation is performed outside WMSD. Sales payment collection, deposits, receivables, debt, full accounting, statutory e-invoicing and live MISA integration are excluded. A general-purpose function to create or edit business policies is not implemented.

 2.4 Stocktake and Inventory Adjustment (BF-04)

+Trigger: Store Owner plans and creates a stocktake session, defining its scope and assigned counters.
 
 End condition: The stocktake is completed when no variance is identified, or an approved inventory adjustment is posted for verified discrepancies. Inventory balances and adjustment history reflect the approved results.
 
@@ -88,43 +89,43 @@

 
 Figure 4. Stocktake and Inventory Adjustment — actor/system workflow.
 
-Decision handling: The system compares physical counts with the book balance captured at the stocktake cut-off. If no variance is detected, the stocktake is marked as completed without an inventory adjustment. If a variance exists, staff performs a recount, records the variance cause and submits an adjustment request. Rejected adjustments do not change inventory balances. Approved adjustments update the inventory and preserve before/after quantities in the adjustment history.
-
-2.5 Warehouse Issue and Stock Deduction (BF-05)
-
-Trigger: An authorized user creates a goods issue request specifying the source reference, products and requested quantities for warehouse fulfillment.
-
-End condition: The actual issued quantities are confirmed, the corresponding inventory is deducted from the selected lots and storage locations, and the goods issue status and inventory movement history are updated.
+Decision handling: Inventory Staff activates counting with an immutable book snapshot, records count/recount attempts and reconciles relevant post-cutoff movements. No discrepancy completes the session without an adjustment. A discrepancy requires an Inventory Staff adjustment request, Store Owner approval and separate Store Owner Clear Difference. Approval does not post stock; only authorized Clear Difference posts the approved version once.
+
+2.5 Sales Order Fulfilment and Internal Invoice (BF-05)
+
+Trigger: Sales Staff creates a Sales Order and an Outbound Delivery for eligible outstanding quantities.
+
+End condition: Inventory Staff confirms picking into staging and posts actual Goods Issue once. Accountant creates and issues the internal invoice for eligible unbilled quantities. SMTP notifications follow committed status events.
 
  
 
 Figure 5. Warehouse Issue and Stock Deduction — actor/system workflow.
 
-Decision handling: Before confirming a goods issue, the system validates usable inventory by lot and location. If sufficient eligible stock is available, the confirmed quantity is deducted and the issue is recorded. If stock is insufficient, the system displays the shortage and requires the warehouse operator to revise the lot selection or issued quantity. Inventory deduction must not proceed until the revised quantities pass validation.
+Decision handling: Validate usable stock, lot/location and outstanding delivery quantities. Picking TO confirmation moves stock to staging without deducting the warehouse total. PGI deducts actual dispatched stock once. Invoice creation/issuance must prevent duplicate billing; neither action collects payment or creates a debt ledger.
 
 2.6 Customer Return and Returned Goods Classification (BF-06)
 
-Trigger: An authorized user records a customer return against an originating transaction, specifying the returned products, lots, quantities and return reasons.
-
-End condition: Returned goods have been inspected and classified, and an approved disposition has been applied. Reusable quantities are returned to usable inventory, while non-reusable quantities remain isolated under the appropriate stock status.
+Trigger: Inventory Staff creates a customer-return request linked to the original sale/issue for Store Owner review before inspection.
+
+End condition: Inventory Staff records inspection/classification and confirms accepted returned quantities. Accountant may request a refund; Store Owner approves it; Accountant records externally completed refund evidence. The original invoice remains immutable.
 
  
 
 Figure 6. Customer Return and Returned Goods Classification — actor/system workflow.
 
-Decision handling: Returned goods are initially kept outside usable inventory pending inspection and disposition approval. If the proposed disposition is rejected, the returned quantity remains isolated and no usable inventory is increased. If approved, reusable goods are transferred to usable inventory, while damaged or non-reusable goods remain isolated under the corresponding status. Inventory changes must follow the approved classification.
+Decision handling: Only accepted quantities are posted according to inspection quality status. Refund eligibility is bounded by accepted return quantities and evidence of the original external payment, less prior or pending refunds. No sales-payment collection or bank transfer is executed in WMSD.
 
 2.7 Supplier Return and Inventory Reconciliation (BF-07)
 
 Trigger: An authorized user creates a supplier return request for goods that must be returned to the supplier, referencing the original receipt, product, lot, storage location, quantity and return reason.
 
-End condition: An approved supplier return is dispatched and completed, with the corresponding stock quantity deducted and its movement history recorded. Rejected returns are documented, and the affected goods remain subject to reclassification and appropriate inventory status handling.
+End condition: Inventory Staff records supplier acceptance, actual dispatch and acknowledgement. Store Owner reviews the reconciled result and closes the supplier return. Unresolved differences remain open.
 
  
 
 Figure 7. Supplier Return and Inventory Reconciliation — actor/system workflow.
 
-Decision handling: Goods identified for supplier return are isolated and excluded from usable inventory while awaiting approval. If the return is approved, the system records dispatch, deducts the corresponding supplier-return inventory and completes the return with traceable movement history. If rejected, the system records the rejection, requires reclassification of the isolated goods and updates their inventory status without posting a supplier-return dispatch.
+Decision handling: Isolate affected stock, record external supplier acceptance and dispatch only eligible quantities. Do not deduct goods again if they never entered owned inventory. Store Owner closure requires reconciled quantities and acknowledgement.

 4. User Requirements
 
 4.1 Actors
 
 # | Actor | Description
-1 | Store Manager | Creates/maintains products and approves replenishment, defective goods, transfer differences, stocktake adjustments and returns. Views applicable reports.
-2 | Accountant | Maintains suppliers, replenishment requests, purchase orders, supplier confirmations and the documented purchasing/receiving views.
-3 | Warehouse Staff | Records receipts, inspections, put-away, quality states, stock movement, stocktakes, actual issues and returns; accesses lot traceability.
-4 | Sales Staff | Views permitted product/stock data, records dispatch requests and customer returns in the source-defined scope.
-5 | Administrator | Manages accounts, roles and access permissions; is not automatically assigned operational warehouse permissions.
+1 | Store Owner | Business approval, master data, stocktake session and Clear Difference.
+2 | Accountant | Purchasing, internal invoice and externally completed refund evidence.
+3 | Inventory Staff | Warehouse receiving, storage, TO, PGI, returns and count execution.
+4 | Sales Staff | Customer reference, Sales Order and Outbound Delivery.
+5 | Administrator | Accounts, permissions and technical configuration; no implied business approval.

 5.3 Background Job Inventory
 
 # | Job Name | Group | Description

 II. Use Case Specifications
 
-1. Access and Role Management (FE-01)
-
-1.1 UC-01: Sign in to the system
-
-Primary Actors | Store Manager, Accountant, Warehouse Staff, Sales Staff, Administrator | Secondary Actors | None specified by the UC source
-Description | As an authorized Store Manager/Accountant/Warehouse Staff/Sales Staff/Administrator user, I want to sign in to the system so that access authorized functions according to the assigned account and role.
-Related feature/screen: FE-01 — Access and Role Management / SC-01.
-Preconditions | Access & identity: An account exists; authentication is initiated by the entered credentials.
-Source records / workflow state: The account exists and has permission to use the system.
-Required inputs (business minimum): Username; password.
-Optional or source-undefined inputs: None.
-Validation and uniqueness: Credentials match an active account; role rights checked.
-Technical field specification: Apply the SQL Server field definitions, precision, field entry requirements and validations in Part III for the linked screen. These are technical design defaults; additional business rules require a separately approved change.
-Normal Flow | 1. An authorized store manager/accountant/warehouse staff/sales staff/administrator user opens SC-01 and selects “Sign in to the system”.
-2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.
-3. Enter username and password on the Sign In screen
-4. System verifies the credentials and checks account activation
-5. System determines permissions assigned to the authenticated role
-6. System starts the authorized user session and opens the permitted work area.
-7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.
-8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.
-9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-08 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.
-A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.
-A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.
-A4 — Business-specific exception: Invalid login, inactive user or denied account returns no session.
-A5 — At source-defined 3: Missing or incorrect credentials: reject sign-in with a non-disclosing error message.
-A5 — At source-defined 3: The account is inactive or unauthorized: prevent access without changing business data.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: An authorized session is established with the assigned role only.
-No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.
-Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.
-Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.
-
-1.2 UC-02: View account list
-
-Primary Actors | Administrator | Secondary Actors | None specified by the UC source
-Description | As a Administrator, I want to view account list so that search and filter accounts by name, role, and active status.
-Related feature/screen: FE-01 — Access and Role Management / SC-02.
-Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Account list under UC-02.
-Reference data: Account records and role descriptions.
-Optional search/filter inputs: Name, role, active/inactive status. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.
-Display fields: Account identifier, staff name, role, status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.
-Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.
-Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-02 and selects “View account list”.
-2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.
-3. The actor optionally enters filters: Name, role, active/inactive status.
-4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.
-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Account identifier, staff name, role, status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.
-A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.
-A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.
-A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.
-A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Account list and its relevant Account identifier, staff name, role, status.
-Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.
-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.
-
-1.3 UC-03: View account details
-
-Primary Actors | Administrator | Secondary Actors | None specified by the UC source
-Description | As a Administrator, I want to view account details so that display the selected account's information, assigned role, and status.
-Related feature/screen: FE-01 — Access and Role Management / SC-02.
-Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Selected account under UC-03.
-Reference data: Account exists.
-Required navigation input: Account identifier. A specific record must be selected before its details can be displayed.
-Display fields: Staff name, role, account status, permitted operations. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.
-Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.

-3. The actor selects the target Selected account by Account identifier.
-4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.
-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Staff name, role, account status, permitted operations with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.

-A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Selected account and its relevant Staff name, role, account status, permitted operations.
-Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.
-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

-Optional or source-undefined inputs: Description; brand; classification.
-Validation and uniqueness: Do not rewrite past transactions or alter existing lot identities through a master edit.
-Technical field specification: Apply the SQL Server field definitions, precision, field entry requirements and validations in Part III for the linked screen. These are technical design defaults; additional business rules require a separately approved change.
-Normal Flow | 1. A store manager opens SC-03 and selects “Update product”.
-2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.
-3. The actor selects the appropriate create/update/activation operation in SC-03.

-5. The actor enters required business fields and any applicable optional values.
-6. The system validates field completeness, references, workflow status and record identity.

-9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.

-A4 — Business-specific exception: Conflicting identifier/conversion changes affecting posted quantities are blocked.

-Display fields: Request number, products, quantity, decision. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.
-Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.
-Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-06 and selects “View replenishment request list”.
-2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.
-3. The actor optionally enters filters: Creation date, creator, status, approval decision.
-4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.
-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Request number, products, quantity, decision with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.
-A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.
-A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.
-A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.
-A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Replenishment list and its relevant Request number, products, quantity, decision.
-Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.
-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

-6.1 UC-41: View warehouse and location list

-Description | As an authorized Store Manager/Warehouse Staff user, I want to view warehouse and location list so that search by warehouse, area, aisle, rack, and location code.
-Related feature/screen: FE-06 — Warehouse and Storage Location Management / SC-09.
-Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Warehouse/location list under UC-41.
-Reference data: Warehouse/location records exist.
-Optional search/filter inputs: Warehouse, area, location code, status. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.
-Display fields: Warehouse, location code, rack, current status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.
-Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.
-Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-09 and selects “View warehouse and location list”.
-2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.
-3. The actor optionally enters filters: Warehouse, area, location code, status.
-4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.
-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Warehouse, location code, rack, current status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.
-A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.
-A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.
-A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.
-A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Warehouse/location list and its relevant Warehouse, location code, rack, current status.
-Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.
-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

-Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

-7.1 UC-47: View stock by lot, location, and quality status
-
-Primary Actors | Store Manager, Accountant, Warehouse Staff, Sales Staff | Secondary Actors | None specified by the UC source
-Description | As an authorized Store Manager/Accountant/Warehouse Staff/Sales Staff user, I want to view stock by lot, location, and quality status so that filter inventory by product, warehouse, lot, tone, calibre, and quality status.
-Related feature/screen: FE-07 — Inventory by Lot, Location and Status / SC-10.
-Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Inventory balance list under UC-47.
-Reference data: Inventory balances exist.
-Optional search/filter inputs: Warehouse, product, lot, location, quality status. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.
-Display fields: Lot, location, actual/usable qty, status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.
-Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.
-Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-10 and selects “View stock by lot, location, and quality status”.
-2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.
-3. The actor optionally enters filters: Warehouse, product, lot, location, quality status.
-4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.
-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Lot, location, actual/usable qty, status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.
-A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.
-A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.
-A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.
-A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.
-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Inventory balance list and its relevant Lot, location, actual/usable qty, status.
-Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.
-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

-5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.
-6. The system displays Snapshot, counts, variance, adjustment decision with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.
-7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
-Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-08 and reveal no protected data.
-A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.
-A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.

-Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Stocktake and adjustment report and its relevant Snapshot, counts, variance, adjustment decision.

-Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+1.3 UC-03: Create replenishment request

A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. A accountant opens SC-06 and selects “Create replenishment request”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Accountant reviews available stock and identifies replenishment demand4. Select product lines, requested quantities and reasons5. System records reference stock and the creator6. Accountant submits the request for Store Owner approval.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Missing line/reason or nonpositive quantity blocks submission.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: Replenishment request is stored for Store Owner decision; no PO/receipt/stock yet.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

+Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-04 and reveal no protected data.A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.A6 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Selected goods receipt and its relevant Physical quantities and accepted/pending/defective/rejected parts.Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+1.10 UC-10: Create goods receipt from purchase order
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Record each supplier delivery separately and link it to its purchase order.Feature: FE-01; Screen: SC-08.
+Preconditions | Access & identity: Actor is signed in and explicitly authorized for UC-10.Source records / workflow state: Purchase order exists and delivery is physically presented for receipt.Required inputs (business minimum): Source PO; delivery identifier / receipt reference; delivered line(s).Optional or source-undefined inputs: Carrier note / external document reference.Validation and uniqueness: The delivery is captured independently; no automatic accepted stock increase.Technical field specification: Use the MongoDB field contract and server-side validations for the linked screen.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. A inventory staff opens SC-08 and selects “Create goods receipt from purchase order”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Inventory Staff selects a purchase order and identifies the current delivery4. System displays relevant order lines and quantity outstanding5. Staff creates a distinct goods receipt for the delivery6. System links the new receipt to its purchase order without posting accepted stock yet.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Duplicate delivery reference is flagged for checking rather than double recording.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: A receipt document is linked to the PO and awaits physical count and inspection.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

+3. Goods Receiving Quality Control (FE-03)
+
+3.1 UC-26: Inspect and Post Goods Receipt
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Inspect the draft receipt and post the actual accepted and segregated quantities in one inspection workflow.Feature: FE-03; Screen: SC-08.
+Preconditions | Active authenticated account; operation permission and record/warehouse scope checked on the server. Referenced documents exist in an eligible state.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Open the draft receipt linked to its PO.2. Enter actual quantity, lot, tone, calibre, condition, inspection classification and discrepancy evidence in the receipt lines.3. Reconcile received quantities; separate accepted, pending, damaged and rejected goods.4. Confirm receipt posting once to the receiving/interim location and correct quality status; update outstanding PO quantity.5. Create or select the subsequent put-away task; pending/nonusable goods remain unavailable.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Short, excess, damaged or mismatched goods: record the variance and evidence within this UC; do not release unaccepted stock.A2 — Duplicate confirmation or changed PO balance: reject or return the already posted result without another receipt.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | The described outcome and actor/time/source references are saved. No stock movement occurs unless explicitly stated in the flow.

+4. Bin-level Inventory and Location Movement (FE-04)
+
+4.1 UC-27: View warehouse and location list
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner, Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Search by warehouse, area, aisle, rack, and location code.Feature: FE-04; Screen: SC-09.
+Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Warehouse/location list under UC-27.Reference data: Warehouse/location records exist.Optional search/filter inputs: Warehouse, area, location code, status. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.Display fields: Warehouse, location code, rack, current status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-09 and selects “View warehouse and location list”.2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.3. The actor optionally enters filters: Warehouse, area, location code, status.4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.6. The system displays Warehouse, location code, rack, current status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-04 and reveal no protected data.A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.A6 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Warehouse/location list and its relevant Warehouse, location code, rack, current status.Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+4.10 UC-36: Create location movement

A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. A inventory staff opens SC-12 and selects “Create location movement”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Inventory Staff identifies a lot, source location and destination location4. Verify that both locations belong to the same warehouse5. Record intended movement quantity6. System validates eligible source stock and creates the movement record.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Cross-warehouse destination requires transfer UC-40.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: Location movement document created, not yet final stock effect.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

+4.11 UC-37: Confirm actual location movement
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Decrease source-location quantity and increase destination-location quantity without changing warehouse total.Feature: FE-04; Screen: SC-12.
+Preconditions | Access & identity: Actor is signed in and explicitly authorized for UC-37.Source records / workflow state: Movement exists; physical operation has taken place.Required inputs (business minimum): Prepared location movement; actual moved quantity.Optional or source-undefined inputs: Execution note.Validation and uniqueness: Actual qty >0 and <= source usable stock; active destination same warehouse.Technical field specification: Use the MongoDB field contract and server-side validations for the linked screen.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. A inventory staff opens SC-12 and selects “Confirm actual location movement”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Inventory Staff opens a pending location movement4. Enter actual moved quantity by lot5. System checks source available balance and active destination6. System subtracts from source and adds to destination atomically without changing warehouse total.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Insufficient stock / repeated confirmation prevents posting.A5 — At source-defined 3: Destination belongs to another warehouse: require the warehouse-transfer flow instead.A6 — At source-defined 3: Source balance is insufficient: reject confirmation.A7 — Duplicate or interrupted posting: do not repeat the quantity change; retain or report the existing posted result.A8 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: Equal source reduction and destination increase; total warehouse unchanged.Data integrity: The exact linked lot/location and quantity/status change is reflected in stock and UC-100–UC-102 audit history.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.

+Alternative Flows | A1 — Invalid input, inactive reference or insufficient permission: reject without changing business data.A2 — Concurrent update or duplicate confirmation: re-read the current state; never repeat a posting.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | The described outcome and actor/time/source references are saved. No stock movement occurs unless explicitly stated in the flow.

+5.6 UC-43: Confirm Warehouse Transfer Order
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Confirm actual warehouse transfer quantities and handle checkpoint differences within the same workflow.Feature: FE-05; Screen: SC-13.
+Preconditions | Active authenticated account; operation permission and record/warehouse scope checked on the server. Referenced documents exist in an eligible state.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Open an authorized TO created from an approved TR.2. Record actual source dispatch, handover and destination receipt quantities, lot/status and evidence as workflow fields, not standalone UCs.3. For staged dispatch, decrease source stock and record In Transit once; on receipt clear the corresponding In Transit quantity and increase destination stock once.4. Compare actual checkpoints; if differences exist, record findings and a proposed resolution and keep the transfer open.5. After matching receipt or approved resolution execution, confirm accepted quantities and close only when all dispatched quantities are reconciled.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Shortage, wrong lot or damage: retain the unresolved balance, segregate unfit goods and submit the resolution to UC-44.A2 — Rejected resolution: revise the proposal in this UC; do not close or silently write off the difference.A3 — Retried confirmation: return the existing result without duplicate stock movements.A4 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | The described outcome and actor/time/source references are saved. No stock movement occurs unless explicitly stated in the flow.

+6.12 UC-56: View goods issue list
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner, Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Search by source request, issue date, and status.Feature: FE-06; Screen: SC-17.
+Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Goods issue list under UC-56.Reference data: Issue records exist.Optional search/filter inputs: Source request, issue date, status. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.Display fields: Issue no., source request, actual qty, status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-17 and selects “View goods issue list”.2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.3. The actor optionally enters filters: Source request, issue date, status.4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.6. The system displays Issue no., source request, actual qty, status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-04 and reveal no protected data.A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.A6 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Goods issue list and its relevant Issue no., source request, actual qty, status.Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+6.18 UC-62: Issue Internal Invoice and Produce PDF
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner, Accountant | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Issue the internal invoice and provide its PDF/print output.Feature: FE-06; Screen: SC-26.
+Preconditions | Authenticated active user with the named operation permission. Referenced records must exist and be in an eligible state.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.Operation scope: Accountant may issue an eligible draft internal invoice and generate/export/print its PDF. Store Owner may export/print an already issued invoice only; it cannot create or issue an invoice. Permission checks are performed per requested sub-operation.
+Normal Flow | 1. Select Issue or Export/Print and authorize that sub-operation.2. For Issue, Accountant reviews the eligible draft against posted unbilled Goods Issue quantities. Validate uniqueness and prevent duplicate billing.3. Assign the invoice number and freeze its customer, quantity, price and amount snapshots. Generate PDF/print output and queue the SMTP event after commit.4. For Export/Print, Store Owner or Accountant selects an already issued invoice. Return its existing immutable snapshot representation without reissuing, changing amounts or generating another issue event.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Invalid input, permission, state or reference: reject without partially changing data.A2 — Repeated or concurrent confirmation: return the current result or require refresh; never repeat a posting.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | The business outcome, actor, time and source references are saved. Stock changes only when the normal flow explicitly posts a movement.

+Primary Actors | Store Owner, Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Filter defective goods by product, lot, location, cause, status, and proposed disposition.Feature: FE-07; Screen: SC-11.
+Preconditions | Access: The actor is signed in and authorized to view Defective case list under UC-63.Reference data: Defective cases exist.Optional search/filter inputs: Warehouse, lot, status, reason. No filters are mandatory; the actor may run an unfiltered list/report within permission scope.Display fields: Case no., lot, defect qty, disposition status. Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-11 and selects “View defective goods list”.2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.3. The actor optionally enters filters: Warehouse, lot, status, reason.4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.6. The system displays Case no., lot, defect qty, disposition status with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-04 and reveal no protected data.A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.A6 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Defective case list and its relevant Case no., lot, defect qty, disposition status.Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+Postconditions | The business outcome, actor, time and source references are saved. Stock changes only when the normal flow explicitly posts a movement.

+7.4 UC-66: Approve or reject defective goods disposition
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Review cause and evidence and approve or reject the proposed handling.Feature: FE-07; Screen: SC-11.
+Preconditions | Access & identity: Actor is signed in and explicitly authorized for UC-66.Source records / workflow state: Manager sees defect, quantity and evidence.Required inputs (business minimum): Pending defect disposition; decision.Optional or source-undefined inputs: Reason (required for rejection).Validation and uniqueness: Only pending proposal can be decided; no quantity change on rejection.Technical field specification: Use the MongoDB field contract and server-side validations for the linked screen.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. A store owner opens SC-11 and selects “Approve or reject defective goods disposition”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Store Owner opens a pending defective goods disposition proposal4. Review lot, quantity, cause and supporting evidence5. Choose Approve or Reject and document the rejection rationale6. System records the decision and enables posting only for approved proposals.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Unapproved/duplicate decision is blocked.A5 — At source-defined 4: The proposal is rejected: retain quarantine and prevent disposition posting.A6 — Reject outcome: a rejection reason is mandatory; record rejection and keep the proposed stock operation unposted.A7 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: Approval decision stored; approved proposal enabled for UC-67 only.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

Values are retrieved from saved records; any displayed quantity is reconciled from the corresponding business transactions.Result scope: Access rights and existing document/quality states determine which records can be opened.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. The actor navigates to SC-19 and selects “View supplier return request details”.2. The system checks role authorization and presents the permitted scope of records.3. The actor selects the target Selected supplier return request by Return request no..4. The system validates referenced record identity, supplied filter values and date-range order where applicable.5. The system retrieves matching records using their current persisted links and permissions.6. The system displays Lot/location, original receipt, evidence, decision with the relevant linked source references and recorded statuses.7. The actor may inspect a result or adjust filters; this read-only action does not create, update or post inventory.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Step 2: Actor lacks view permission; show MSG-04 and reveal no protected data.A2 — Step 3/4: A selected record identifier does not exist or is outside authorized scope; show a non-disclosing not-found/permission result.A3 — Step 3/5: No record matches the filters; show MSG-01 and an empty list rather than sample or fabricated rows.A4 — Step 4: Filters conflict (for example, From Date after To Date) or have an invalid value; highlight the filter, leave the stored data unchanged.A5 — Step 5/6: A linked document is unavailable or historical/inactive; preserve the original reference and present only data for which the actor has access.A6 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: The actor has seen the Selected supplier return request and its relevant Lot/location, original receipt, evidence, decision.Persisted business data: No creation, status transition, inventory change or approval occurs from viewing or filtering.Empty/error outcome: No record is modified; the user can revise filters or return to the list.

+8.3 UC-86: Create Stocktake Session
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Plan the stocktake and create its session, covering business-flow steps 1 and 2.Feature: FE-08; Screen: SC-14.
+Preconditions | Authenticated Store Owner with operation permission. Warehouse, locations and lot/quality scope exist.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Plan the warehouse/area, included locations/lots/statuses, intended count time and assigned Inventory Staff.2. Validate nonempty scope, active references and conflicting open sessions for the same stock scope.3. Create the stocktake session with planned scope and counting assignment.4. Make the session available to assigned Inventory Staff; the immutable book-quantity snapshot is captured when counting starts under UC-87.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Missing or inconsistent scope: retain the draft and show validation errors.A2 — A conflicting active session exists: require resolution before activating another count of the same scope.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Session created; no inventory adjustment or standalone snapshot action is performed. Planning is part of this UC, not another document or permission.
+
+8.4 UC-87: Perform Stocktake Count and Record Results
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Freeze book quantity at count activation, perform physical counting and record results; count and recount share one UC with attempt history. Covers steps 3 and 4.Feature: FE-08; Screen: SC-14.
+Preconditions | Authenticated Inventory Staff assigned to the session and warehouse. Session is created/open and has not been closed or cleared.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Open the assigned session and start counting. At the defined cutoff, automatically capture the immutable book quantity by warehouse, location, product, lot and quality status.2. Perform the physical count and record actual quantities in the permitted units, including explicit zero quantities for counted empty lines.3. Save each result with counter, time, attempt number, note/evidence and product-specific unit conversion.4. If recount is needed, append a new attempt under this same UC; retain every previous attempt and identify the result selected for reconciliation.5. Submit the completed count results to UC-88. Do not adjust stock while entering results.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Snapshot already exists: reuse it; repeated activation must not overwrite the cutoff baseline.A2 — Negative quantity, invalid conversion or missing count line: block submission. A blank value is not zero.A3 — Movements after cutoff: preserve them separately and reconcile their timing; do not overwrite the snapshot or treat their quantities as unexplained stock loss.A4 — Closed session or results already included in an approved adjustment: prevent silent edits; approval must never reference a changed count version.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Immutable snapshot and versioned count/recount attempts exist; stock balances are unchanged. Freeze Book Qty freezes the comparison baseline, not a blanket lock on every warehouse transaction.
+
+8.5 UC-88: Reconcile Stocktake
+
+Primary Actors | Inventory Staff | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Compare the finalized physical count with the frozen book quantity, investigate reasons and follow the discrepancy decision in step 5.Feature: FE-08; Screen: SC-14.
+Preconditions | Authenticated assigned Inventory Staff. Snapshot and complete submitted count results exist; relevant movement timing can be reconciled.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Review the selected count/recount attempt for each lot/location/status and normalize units.2. Compare like-for-like quantities at the cutoff; identify intervening movements so the comparison is not between different times.3. Record differences, reasons and supporting evidence within this UC. If necessary, return to UC-87 for another count attempt.4. If no discrepancy remains, mark the session reconciled and close it without creating an adjustment request or stock movement.5. If a discrepancy remains, preserve the reconciled difference/version and proceed to UC-91; the session remains open.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Incomplete count or unresolved movement timing: keep the session open and do not allow adjustment submission.A2 — A selected count result changes: invalidate the prior reconciliation and recompute before submitting a request.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | No difference: session closed with no adjustment. Difference: traceable reconciled variance awaits a request. Reconciliation itself never posts stock.
+

+8.9 UC-92: Approve Inventory Adjustment
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Review and approve the documented difference in step 7; approval does not clear stock.Feature: FE-08; Screen: SC-15.
+Preconditions | Authenticated Store Owner. Pending request references current reconciled count version and complete evidence.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Review book snapshot, count/recount history, intervening movements, variance reason and proposed signed adjustment.2. Verify the requested quantity matches the supported difference and the request has not already been cleared.3. Approve the exact request/version and save approver and timestamp.4. Make the approved request available for the separate Store Owner Clear Difference action in UC-93.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Insufficient evidence or inconsistent quantities: do not approve or post; retain an unresolved request.A2 — Request already approved/cleared or its evidence version changed: reject the repeated/stale operation.A3 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Approved request is eligible for clearing; inventory is unchanged. The supplied flow defines an approval path, not an additional business rejection-and-rework branch.
+
+8.10 UC-93: Clear Stocktake Difference
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Execute step 8 by posting the approved difference and closing the reconciled stocktake.Feature: FE-08; Screen: SC-15.
+Preconditions | Authenticated Store Owner. Adjustment is approved, version-consistent and unposted; all relevant differences have a documented disposition.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.
+Normal Flow | 1. Open the approved adjustment and verify approval, scope, signed quantity and unposted state.2. Apply the approved signed difference to the correct current lot/location/quality balance. Preserve valid movements after cutoff; never replace current stock blindly with the old physical count.3. Atomically save the updated balance, stock movement, before/after values, actor/time and posted adjustment reference.4. Mark the difference cleared and close the stocktake only when no unresolved differences remain.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — Inventory Staff or any other non-owner attempts clearing: deny the operation even if the request is approved.A2 — No approval, stale version or unresolved scope: block posting without partial changes.A3 — Duplicate/retried confirmation: return the existing posted outcome; do not apply the difference twice.A4 — Conflicting stock update or invalid resulting balance: rollback the coupled operation and require a fresh validated retry.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Approved difference is posted once and recorded in the stock movement log. Store Owner, not Inventory Staff, clears the difference. Session closes after all differences are resolved.

+9.1 UC-94: Sign in to the system
+
+Primary Actors | Store Owner, Accountant, Inventory Staff, Sales Staff, Administrator | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Access authorized functions according to the assigned account and role.Feature: FE-09; Screen: SC-01.
+Preconditions | Access & identity: An account exists; authentication is initiated by the entered credentials.Source records / workflow state: The account exists and has permission to use the system.Required inputs (business minimum): Username; password.Optional or source-undefined inputs: None.Validation and uniqueness: Credentials match an active account; role rights checked.Technical field specification: Use the MongoDB field contract and server-side validations for the linked screen.
+Normal Flow | 1. An authorized store owner/accountant/inventory staff/sales staff/administrator user opens SC-01 and selects “Sign in to the system”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Enter username and password on the Sign In screen4. System verifies the credentials and checks account activation5. System determines permissions assigned to the authenticated role6. System starts the authorized user session and opens the permitted work area.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Invalid login, inactive user or denied account returns no session.A5 — At source-defined 3: Missing or incorrect credentials: reject sign-in with a non-disclosing error message.A6 — At source-defined 3: The account is inactive or unauthorized: prevent access without changing business data.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: An authorized session is established with the assigned role only.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

+Postconditions | The business outcome, actor, time and source references are saved. Stock changes only when the normal flow explicitly posts a movement.

+9.5 UC-98: Assign roles and operation permissions
+
+Primary Actors | Administrator | Secondary Actors | None
+Description | Configure view, create, edit, and transaction-recording permissions by role.Feature: FE-09; Screen: SC-02.
+Preconditions | Access & identity: Actor is signed in and explicitly authorized for UC-98.Source records / workflow state: Administrator can access known role and UC operation definitions.Required inputs (business minimum): Role; one or more operation permissions.Optional or source-undefined inputs: Permission removal/addition reason.Validation and uniqueness: Cannot assign a role or business operation absent from approved actor/UC catalogue.Technical field specification: Use the MongoDB field contract and server-side validations for the linked screen.Authorization: Permission is checked for this exact action and record on the server. A list/detail permission does not grant create, update, approve or post rights. Inventory Staff is limited to assigned warehouses; Administrator account/role maintenance does not grant business-operation rights.Operation scope: Administrator assigns only the five approved roles and the operation permissions defined in this specification. It cannot create additional business roles, arbitrary business policies, approval chains or stock-posting rules, or perform business approvals through this administrative UC.
+Normal Flow | 1. A administrator opens SC-02 and selects “Assign roles and operation permissions”.2. The system checks permission, displays the relevant form and current source values.3. Administrator opens an internal account or role-permissions configuration4. System displays available business operations and current role assignments5. Administrator selects the role and its permitted view/create/update/post actions6. System validates and saves the permission configuration used by subsequent operations.7. The system validates all mandatory input fields, cross-document quantities, actor authorization and referenced record state before accepting confirmation.8. On success the system saves the specific business outcome and updates related documents or eligible stock only if this UC explicitly performs a stock-posting operation.9. The system displays the resulting record/status and source references; inventory-affecting operations retain the before/after movement details.
+Alternative Flows | A1 — At step 2, access/role check fails: show MSG-04 and reveal no protected record; no data are changed.A2 — At step 3–6, a required input is absent: identify the specific missing field (MSG-02); retain entered information without committing.A3 — At the validation step 7, a reference is inactive, duplicate, outside the current workflow stage or violates the business rule: explain the specific field/record; do not partially post.A4 — Business-specific exception: Invalid or unknown operations and insufficient privilege are rejected.A5 — Permission denied: Reject an unauthorized sub-operation, direct request, record or warehouse scope without changing data; show MSG-04.
+Postconditions | Successful outcome: Subsequent authorization checks use saved operation-level permissions.No inventory balance is changed unless the described UC expressly authorizes a stock movement.Failure outcome: Missing inputs, a rejected action or a failed validation leave existing posted transactions and inventory unchanged.Traceability: Existing source references, responsible actor and action time are retained where this UC creates or updates a business record.

 III. Functional Requirements
 
-Every list mockup displays illustrative records in its on-screen results table. Mockup data do not represent customer operational records. The Field Name / Description table below each screen specifies recommended Microsoft SQL Server types, lengths/precision, entry requirements, defaults and validations. A required field must be populated in the stated workflow step; an Optional filter does not persist to a transaction; a Read-only field is populated from existing records or calculations. Technical data type recommendations do not introduce unapproved workflows or approval rules.
-

 Field Name | Description

-Recount Quantity | DECIMAL(18,3). Entry: Optional recount. Default: NULL.Validation: Optional physical recount; retain original count and user/time rather than overwriting it.
-Variance | DECIMAL(18,3). Entry: System-calculated. Default: Assigned by the workflow (no fixed SQL DEFAULT).Validation: Signed difference between a physical amount and its comparison baseline; can be positive, zero or negative.
-Cause | NVARCHAR(500). Entry: Required when documenting a variance. Default: No SQL DEFAULT; supply or select a value.Validation: Explain the discrepancy or damage cause; required for recorded defect/variance resolution where specified.

 1. External Interfaces
 
 Interface | Requirement | Acceptance / Verification
-Web user interface | Provide feature-specific forms, lists, filters and confirmation actions with role-appropriate access; clearly distinguish product, lot, tone, calibre, location and quality state. | Review SC-01 through SC-21 and ensure relevant fields are visible without confusing quality or quantity types.

 2.4 Reliability and Data Integrity
 
-Posting of accepted receipts, intra-warehouse movement, transfer dispatch/receipt, approved adjustment, actual issue and supplier return must be atomic at the document/line level. Repeated confirmation or interrupted operations must not increase or decrease the same eligible quantity more than once. This describes the required business outcome; it does not prescribe a particular database technology.
+MongoDB is the persistence technology. Stock/document postings require atomic and idempotent outcomes and concurrency checks. Picking confirmation, PGI, receipt, transfer, refund recording and Clear Difference must not repeat their business effect when retried. Preserve posted movement and invoice snapshots.
 
 2.5 Maintainability and Traceability
 
-Each requirement retains its FE/UC identifier and is mapped to a screen and a fully specified use case. Data-rule changes must be assessed for impacts on unit conversion, source document linkage, approval steps and movement history. A revision must never silently rewrite the meaning of an existing UC ID.
+Each active UC maps to one of nine features, its screen, permission row and specification. Enforce permissions at operation and record/warehouse scope. Fixed business rules are implemented through the defined workflows; users do not create or edit arbitrary policies.

 3. Other Requirements
+
+General-purpose policy creation/editing is outside scope. The application implements only the specified fixed validations, workflow decisions and five-role operation permissions. Maintaining selling-price reference data or assigning existing permissions does not introduce a policy-management module. Sales payment collection, deposits, receivables, full accounting and statutory e-invoice transmission are excluded; accepted-return refunds are approved and recorded as externally completed transactions.
